# Altair Inspire — official Help index (2026 current site)

This generated catalog maps **1,590 index terms** to **3,028 official page references** (786 distinct page paths). It is a navigation/index layer, not a replacement copy of the Help pages.

Source: [https://help.altair.com/inspire/en_us/indexTerms.htm](https://help.altair.com/inspire/en_us/indexTerms.htm) (HTTP 200; page title: Index Terms - Altair Inspire User Guide).

For task routing and version-selection rules, start at [Official Help catalog](../README.md). Search this file for the user-facing command, panel, concept, or API name, then open its official page. Entries may point to the same page; keep the source label and release attached when summarizing.
